Book of Light

Effect: Create light within the specified radius.

Induce Calm
Level: 6 Action: Active Type: Spiritual
Effect: Calms individuals feeling fear or hatred within the specified radius
from the caster. Causes any Fear, Terror, or Rage states to disappear,
even if they are supernatural in origin. This spell can be resisted with a
successful MR or PsR check.
Casting Level Base Intermediate Advanced Arcane
Zeon 40 80 120 160
Int. Required 5 8 11 14
Base: 10m Radius, 80 MR or PsR
Intermediate: 25m Radius, 100 MR or PsR
Advanced: 50m Radius, 120 MR or PsR
Arcane: 100m Radius, 140 MR or PsR
Maintenance: None
Blinding Flash
Level: 8 Action: Active Type: Automatic
Effect: Causes a sudden flash of light with the specified radius. It blinds
anyone looking at it when it is cast for as many combat turns as they fail
the PhR check, divided by 10. It is not possible to designate targets within
the area of the flash, and everyone except the caster is equally affected. If
someone is actively avoiding looking at the source of the flash, they
receive a +40 bonus to their resistance.
Casting Level Base Intermediate Advanced Arcane
Zeon 50 100 150 200
Int. Required 6 9 11 14
Base: 10m Radius, 140 PhR
Intermediate: 25m Radius, 140 PhR
Advanced: 50m Radius, 140 PhR
Arcane: 100m Radius, 160 PhR
Maintenance: None
Shield of Light
Level: 10 Action: Passive Type: Defense
Effect: Forms a barrier of Energy that protects the caster from any source
of attack. The shield has a number of Life Points based on the spell level
when cast, but is only damaged by supernatural attacks. Any attack based
on Darkness causes double damage.
Casting Level Base Intermediate Advanced Arcane
Zeon 50 120 180 250
Int. Required 6 8 10 14
Base: 300 Life Points
Intermediate: 1000 Life Points
Advanced: 1800 Life Points
Arcane: 3000 Life Points
Maintenance: [5 / 15 / 20 / 25]
Perceive
Level: 12 Action: Active Type: Effect
Effect: Improves the perception of the caster, increasing their secondary
abilities of Notice and Search by the specified amount. Their Magic
Appraisal also increases by this amount, but only for the purposes of
detecting or measuring magical potency, not to hide it.

Armor of Light
Level: 16 Action: Active Type: Effect
Effect: Forms a layer of mystical armor with an AT against all types of
damage, and a higher value for attacks on the Energy table. Although this
counts as a layer of armor, it does not count as an additional layer for the
purposes of initiative penalties.

Arcane: 6 AT, 12 Energy AT

Banish Shadows
Level: 18 Action: Active Type: Spiritual, Effect
Effect: Destroys shadows within the specified radius. Any Darkness based
creatures within the radius must pass a MR check or lose double their
failure level in Life Points (Damage Resistant creatures increase this
amount by their Damage Resistance Multiple). As long as the spell is
maintained over the creatures, they must make a new MR check each
combat turn.

Level: 1-30
Hologram
Level: 26 Action: Active Type: Effect
Effect: Creates an immaterial luminous form with a maximum size
determined by the level of the spell. The caster can give the hologram any
appearance they desire, making it very difficult to differentiate it from
something real. If they create a creature, it can perform any inhuman
action the caster wishes, but will mimic the physical abilities of the caster.
If, for example, the hologram is used to simulate an attack, it uses the
combat ability of the caster. The hologram cannot touch anyone nor be
touched, but if it receives any damage based on energy, it disappears. To
detect the hologram is not real, it is necessary to achieve an Almost
Impossible (240) Notice check, or a Very Difficult (140) Search check.
Casting Level Base Intermediate Advanced Arcane
Zeon 40 150 250 350
Int. Required 6 9 12 15
Base: 1m2
Intermediate: 15m2
Advanced: 50m2
Arcane: 100m2
Maintenance: [5 / 20 / 25 / 30]
Bonds of Light
Level: 28 Action: Active Type: Attack
Effect: Creates bonds of light that hold the designated target immobile.
An attack is made using the rules for Trapping, although the caster suffers
no penalty to their ability for performing this maneuver. The bonds use a
strength value for the Trapping check based on the level of the spell, and
are treated as an Energy based item with a fortitude of 25.
Casting Level Base Intermediate Advanced Arcane
Zeon 60 100 140 180
Int. Required 6 9 12 15
Base: 8 Strength
Intermediate: 12 Strength
Advanced: 15 Strength, Fortitude increases to 30.
Arcane: 18 Strength, Fortitude increases to 35.
Maintenance: [10 / 10 / 15 / 15]
Control Light
Level: 30 Action: Active Type: Spiritual, Effect
Effect: Modifies and controls the form, color, or intensity of light in the
specified radius. If any Light based beings are in the area, they must pass a
MR check with difficulty determined by the level of the spell, or fall under
control of the caster. A creature can only repeat the check if ordered to do
something against its nature.

Level: 1-40
Spy of Light
Level: 36 Action: Active Type: Effect
Effect: Creates a small energy creature of light that moves as the caster
wishes, with a flight value of 14. The maximum distance it can travel is
determined by the level of the spell. Through it, the caster can see and hear
as though they were present physically, but doing so consumes their
senses, and they can only see the world through the Spy of Light. Each
combat turn, the caster decides whether to use their own senses, or the Spy
of Light. The spy of light has a separate Secondary Ability value for Notice
and Search. If attacked, it can defend itself with the Magic Projection of
the caster. For the purposes of initiative, it acts on the same turn as it’s
controller. It is only possible to attack the Spy of Light with an attack
capable of damaging energy, and is destroyed if it receives any damage.
2

Advanced: 200 Notice and Search, 50km distance
Arcane: 250 Notice and Search, 500km distance
Maintenance: [20 / 40 / 60 / 80] Daily
Ecstasy
Level: 38 Action: Active Type: Spiritual
Effect: Intoxicates anyone in the affected area with a feeling of utter
ecstasy. The sensations are so powerful that the victim’s senses are
completely clouded, and they receive a -20 all action penalty while
affected. However, the same effect makes the spell’s victims completely
oblivious and immune to any pain or other affliction based penalty, except
for actually being physically incapacitated. Resisting the spell requires a
successful MR check determined by the level of the spell.
Casting Level Base Intermediate Advanced Arcane
Zeon 60 90 120 150
Int. Required 6 9 12 16

Intermediate: 50m area, 100 MR
Advanced: 100m area, 120 MR
Arcane: 250m area, 160 MR
Maintenance: [10 / 10 / 15 / 15]

Seeking Sphere
Level: 46 Action: Active Type: Attack
Effect: Unleashes a sphere of luminous energy with a base damage
determined by the level of the spell. The caster can control it using their
own magic projection until it hits its target. If the target successfully
dodges, the Seeking Sphere can continue to attack on the following turn, as
it has not been destroyed. When the Seeking Sphere causes damage or is
successfully blocked, it explodes and vanishes. If the caster wishes they
can abandon active control of it, and it will continue to attack
independently with a separate magic projection based on the level of the
spell.
Casting Level Base Intermediate Advanced Arcane
Zeon 120 180 240 300
Int. Required 6 9 12 15
Base: 150 Magic Projection, 100 Base Damage
Intermediate: 180 Magic Projection, 120 Base Damage
Advanced: 210 Magic Projection, 160 Base Damage
Arcane: 240 Magic Projection, 200 Base Damage
Maintenance: [15 / 20 / 25 / 30]
Zone of Detection
Level: 48 Action: Active Type: Detection
Effect: Allows the caster to detect any being within the area of effect that
does not succeed a MR check. The Zone of Detection allows the caster to
determine how many individuals are in the zone, and their exact locations.
It also senses any Detection spells that attempt to enter the area, as long as
the caster using them does not succeed at the MR check (regardless of
actual location). The spell creates the area with a specified radius, and is
stationary in the place it was first cast.
Casting Level Base Intermediate Advanced Arcane
Zeon 140 200 280 360
Int. Required 7 10 12 15
Base: 20m Radius, 180 MR
Intermediate: 80m Radius, 240 MR
Advanced: 150m Radius, 280 MR
Arcane: 250m Radius, 340 MR
Maintenance: [10 / 10 / 15 / 15] Daily
Enter Another’s Dreams
Level: 50 Action: Active Type: Spiritual
Effect: Allows the caster to physically enter a sleeper’s dreams. The caster
has no control over the dream world of the dreamer, and anything that
happens there will be real for the caster. The person must have peaceful
dreams to be affected by this spell, and the moment the dream becomes a
nightmare, or if the target awakens or dies, the caster abandons the dream
world and returns to reality. Any spiritual spell cast on the dreamer while
the caster is present in the dream will also affect the caster. The spell can
be resisted with a successful MR or PsR check. Once in the target’s
dreams, the caster can ‘jump’ to the unconscious of yet another dreamer
who is physically within range, as determined by the level of the spell.
Naturally, this new target will have their own resistance check. If the
dreamer’s consciousness happens to be transported to the wake, the caster
is trapped there even if the spell expires.
Casting Level Base Intermediate Advanced Arcane
Zeon 120 180 240 300
Int. Required 6 9 12 15
Base: 140 MR or PsR, 10m distance
Intermediate: 160 MR or PsR, 80m distance
Advanced: 200 MR or PsR, 140m distance
Arcane: 240 MR or PsR, 200m distance
Maintenance: [10 / 10 / 15 / 15] Daily
3
Light Form
Level: 52 Action: Active Type: Effect
Effect: The body designated by the caster is transformed into pure
luminous energy and becomes intangible to physical and non-energy
attacks. While in this state, the transformed person gains a bonus to their
secondary abilities of Notice and Search, and a bonus to resistances against
any effect based on Light. While in this state, any damage caused by
attacks based on Darkness is doubled.
Casting Level Base Intermediate Advanced Arcane
Zeon 100 120 140 160
Int. Required 8 11 13 16
Base: +50 to Notice and Search, +20 to Resistances.
Intermediate: +60 to Notice and Search, +30 to Resistances.
Advanced: As Intermediate, but resistance bonus applies to all effects not
based on Darkness.
Arcane: As Advanced, but doesn’t suffer double damage from Darkness
based attacks.
Maintenance: [10 / 15 / 15 / 20]
Free Access
Level: 1-60

receive an all action bonus, as well as a bonus to all resistances, based on
the level of the spell. All allies within the radius of effect from the caster
are affected. No one can be affected by the effect of more than one
blessing at a time.

See Truth
Level: 60 Action: Active Type: Effect
Effect: Permits the affected person to perceive supernatural forces that are
invisible to the human eye, including magic, psychic matrices, and
invisible or supernatural beings, depending on the level of the spell.
Although this spell does not directly work against illusion spells as they
affect the mind, not the vision of the person, anyone using See Truth
against visual illusions can apply a bonus to their MR or PsR checks,
since it helps detect their falseness.
Casting Level Base Intermediate Advanced Arcane
Zeon 100 120 180 250
Int. Required 8 12 14 16
Base: Allows sight of Magic, Psychic Matrices, and invisible beings,
grants +50 to resistances against illusions.
Intermediate: As Base, but allows sight of Spiritual beings.
Advanced: As Intermediate, but +75 to resistances.
Arcane: As Advanced, but +100 to resistances
Maintenance: [10 / 15 / 15 / 25] Daily
Shield from Negative
Level: 62 Action: Active Type: Automatic
Effect: Enchants a designated area, making it impenetrable for beings
based naturally on negative emotions or Darkness. Any such creature
entering the zone must pass a MR check or suffer loss of Life Points equal
to the margin of failure. Additionally, upon failing the check, such a
creature receives an immediate -40 All Action Penalty. The affected zone
has a radius determined by the level of the spell, and is stationary in the
place it is cast.

Level: 1-70
Find
Level: 66 Action: Active Type: Detection
Effect: The caster can locate any person, place, or thing, and know it’s
exact location in that moment regardless of the distance separating them.
Anything can be found, whether an individual object, a type of object, or
simply something that fulfills a specified condition. For example, the
caster can try to locate a city; the thief who stole his crosier (even if he
doesn’t know who did it), or the closest eligible maiden of royal blood.
Objects, places, or people affected must pass a MR check to avoid being
located. Large locations apply a -40 to penalty to this check.
Casting Level Base Intermediate Advanced Arcane
Zeon 160 200 260 320
Int. Required 8 10 13 16
Base: 140 MR
Intermediate: 180 MR
Advanced: 220 MR
Arcane: 260 MR
Maintenance: None
Restore
Level: 68 Action: Active Type: Effect
Effect: Allows the affected person to recover from any penalties including
those caused by fatigue, hunger, physical damage, and even spells,
although not from actual physical loss of body parts from Critical attacks.
Restore also automatically restores fatigue based on the level of the spell.
Casting Level Base Intermediate Advanced Arcane

4

Intermediate: Eliminates up to -80 penalty, and up to 5 Fatigue.
Advanced: Eliminates up to -120 penalty, and up to 10 Fatigue.
Arcane: Eliminates any level of penalty, Restores all missing Fatigue.
Maintenance: None
Hypnotic Display
Level: 70 Action: Active Type: Automatic
Effect: Creates a spectacle of light in a specified place that has a
fascinating and dumbfounding effect. All characters looking directly at the
display cannot help but continue to watch it. It is effective for a radius
specified by the level of the spell, and anyone seeing it must succeed at a
MR or PsR check to avoid it’s effects. Those successfully affected can
perform passive actions, but cannot move, and can make new resistance
checks every time they are attacked.

Lordship Over Dreams
Level: 80 Action: Active Type: Spiritual, Effect
Effect: Permits control over any type of dream of an individual that does
not succeed at an MR check. The caster will have the ability to control the
dream world of the sleeper as if they were an entity with Gnosis 45. In the
event that the dream becomes one fed by negative energy – that is,
becomes a nightmare, their Gnosis is only 30. Spells that the sorcerer is
able to cast thanks only to their elevated Gnosis (such as divine magic)
have no effect outside the spatial limits of the spell, and their effects vanish
as soon as the caster leaves the dream or the Wake.
Casting Level Base Intermediate Advanced Arcane
Zeon 300 400 500 750
Int. Required 12 14 16 18
Base: 140 MR
Intermediate: 150 MR, If the caster is in the wake, they also control their
surroundings as a being with Gnosis 40, as long as they are in an area fed
by positive energy. In neutral areas, their Gnosis is only 30. This effect
remains stationary to the area of the Wake when cast, and only if there are
no similar entities in the area.
Advanced: 160 MR, As Intermediate, but the caster has Gnosis 35 in
neutral areas.
Arcane: 180 MR, As Advanced, but the caster may influence all positive
areas of the wake simultaneously.
Maintenance: [60 / 65 / 70 / 80]
High Magic
Create Being of Light
Level: 82 Action: Active Type: Effect
Effect: Creates a luminous being with the appearance of life, but
completely under the control of the caster. The entity is developed as a
Being Between Worlds, using the powers and limitations of Light
Elementals explained in Anima: Beyond Fantasy - Chapter 26:
Creation of Beings. This creature has a number of Development Points
(DP) based on the level of the spell, which dictates it’s level for the
purposes of presence and abilities.

Reflecting Prism
Level: 86 Action: Passive Type: Defense
Effect: Creates a body of light that works like a prismatic shield,
reflecting any spell, psychic attack, or Ki technique back at it’s caster. For
the attack to be reflected, Reflecting Prism must successfully block the
attack, and the attack must lose a Spell Clash against a spell with an
equivalent base damage designated by the spell level. If defending against
an area attack, the prism does not reflect the entire spell; it still affects
anyone in the area of the spell except for the holder of the Reflecting
Prism. This spell does not reflect Spiritual spells. The caster can use his
magic projection ability for the reflected attack. Like other supernatural
shields, it has a number of Life Points determined by the level of the spell.
Casting Level Base Intermediate Advanced Arcane
Zeon 160 250 300 400
Int. Required 10 13 15 17
Base: 100 base damage, 800 Life Points
Intermediate: 120 base damage, 1500 Life Points
Advanced: 140 base damage, 3000 Life Points
Arcane: 180 base damage, 6000 Life Points
Maintenance: [20 / 25 / 30 / 40] Daily
Radius of Omniscience
Level: 88 Action: Passive Type: Effect
Effect: Permits the caster to be omniscient concerning any occurrence or
thought within a certain radius of their location. Affects only individuals
under the designated presence whom do not have a higher Gnosis than the
caster. The caster will automatically know everything that is happening
and everything thought by individuals affected within the radius. No
resistance is possible for this effect.

Predict
Level: 90 Action: Active Type: Spiritual
Effect: Permits the caster to foresee future events that can happen around
a given person, place, or thing. Predict will show the caster the most
probable destiny awaiting someone or something, providing detailed
information about future events. The period of time covered is determined
by the level of the spell, The Game Master can truthfully tell the caster
what events will occur in the future. Of course, these predictions are not
infallible; as what is seen is only the most probable destiny, which may be
changed through the intervention of higher powers or the actions of
persons with elevated Gnosis.
Casting Level Base Intermediate Advanced Arcane
Zeon 200 300 450 600

Advanced: 50 years – If predicting something happening within 1 day, it
will be very accurate
Arcane: 100 years – If predicting something happening within 1 year, it
will be very accurate
Maintenance: None
Divine Magic
Prison of Light
Level: 92 Action: Active Type: Spiritual
Effect: Encloses the victim in an inescapable world of light, a separate
universe with no interaction with our own. While the victim is imprisoned,
they will have no knowledge of anything that happens outside the prison,
nor does anyone outside have knowledge of the prisoner.
To break free, the prison is considered a damage resistant creature with AT
10, with a number of life points based on the level of the spell. However,
any damage suffered from the outside is doubled automatically. The prison
additionally recovers from damage with a regeneration value of 19.
If the target of the spell fails an MR check, they are imprisoned and do not
have the opportunity for a subsequent roll. If they wish to leave, they must
break free from the prison.
The caster may not lock themselves into their own Prison of Light.
Casting Level Base Intermediate Advanced Arcane
Zeon 200 350 500 800
Int. Required 14 16 18 20
Base: 10,000 Life Points, 140 MR
Intermediate: 250,000 Life Points, 180 MR
Advanced: 500,000 Life Points, 220 MR
Arcane: The Prison can not be broken from within, but possesses 500,000
Life Points for attacks from the outside, 240 MR
Maintenance: [40 / 70 / 100 / 160]
Free Access
Level: 1-100
One with the Light
Level: 96 Action: Active Type: Effect
Effect: Permits the caster to enter a state of utter meditation where their
body becomes one with the Light. They abandon this world and ascend
into the flow of souls to nourish themselves from its energies, apart from,
and without knowledge of anything happening elsewhere. In this state,
they multiply their Zeon Regeneration rate by 10, and cure their wounds
with a regeneration of 16. Although there is no maintenance cost for this
spell, the caster can be in the flow of souls for a maximum time period
based on the level of the spell.

Holocaust of Light
Level: 100 Action: Active Type: Attack, Spiritual
Effect: Unleashes the power of Light in it’s purest state, sweeping away
everything in the spiritual as well as material world. The freed energy
sweeps up and devours everything, unifying it’s existence to the Light.
Even elemental creatures of light are assimilated by it’s power. The
Holocaust of Light creates a great luminous dome within which everything
is dissolved. It is an Energy attack with damage and radius determined by
the level of the spell. Anyone receiving damage, regardless of the amount,
must pass an MR check with difficulty 160 or be joined with the light, and
automatically be destroyed in both body and soul. It is not possible to
designate targets within the radius of this spell; all except the caster are
equally affected.

Enter Another’s Nightmares
Level: 50 Action: Active Type: Spiritual
Effect: Allows the caster to physically enter a sleeper’s dreams. The
caster has no control over the dream world of the dreamer, and anything
that happens there will be real for the caster. The person must have terrible
nightmares to be affected by this spell, and the moment the dream
becomes peaceful, or if the target awakens or dies, the caster abandons the
9
dream world and returns to reality. Any spiritual spell cast on the dreamer
while the caster is present in the dream will also affect the caster. The spell
can be resisted with a successful MR or PsR check. Once in the target’s
dreams, the caster can ‘jump’ to the unconscious of yet another dreamer
who is physically nearby, as determined by the level of the spell. Naturally,
this new target will have their own resistance check. If the dreamer’s
consciousness happens to be transported to the wake, the caster is trapped
there even if the spell expires.

Prison of Darkness
Level: 92 Action: Active Type: Spiritual
Effect: Encloses the victim in an inescapable world of darkness, a separate
universe with no interaction with our own. While the victim is imprisoned,
they will have no knowledge of anything that happens outside the prison,
nor does anyone outside have knowledge of the prisoner.
To break free, the prison is considered a damage resistant creature with AT
10, with a number of life points based on the level of the spell. However,
any damage suffered from the outside is doubled automatically. The prison
additionally recovers from damage with a regeneration value of 19.
If the target of the spell fails an MR check, they are imprisoned and do not
have the opportunity for a subsequent roll. If they wish to leave, they must
break free from the prison.
The caster may not lock themselves into their own Prison of Darkness.

Holocaust of Darkness
Level: 100 Action: Active Type: Attack, Spiritual
Effect: Unleashes the power of Darkness in it’s purest state, sweeping
away everything in the spiritual as well as material world. The freed
energy sweeps up and devours everything, unifying it’s existence to the
Darkness. Even elemental creatures of Darkness are assimilated by it’s
power. The Holocaust of Darkness creates a great shadowed dome within
which everything is dissolved. It is an Energy attack with damage and
radius determined by the level of the spell. Anyone receiving damage,
regardless of the amount, must pass an MR check of difficulty 160 or be
joined with the dark, and automatically be destroyed in both body and
soul. It is not possible to designate targets within the radius of this spell; all
except the caster are equally affected.

Create Homunculus
Level: 26 Action: Active Type: Effect
Effect: Homunculi are small magical beings that casters use at times for
various purposes, from espionage to housework. The spell creates a
number of mystical beings with 500 Development Points (Level 0), as per
the rules in Anima: Beyond Fantasy - Chapter 26: Creation of Beings
under complete control of the caster. They are a being between worlds with
a Gnosis of 5, but given their minor nature, the following rules apply:
• The characteristics of the being cannot exceed a score of 5.
• Any primary and secondary abilities cannot exceed a score of 50.
• Any Intellectual Secondary ability and Mystical ability chosen
cannot exceed the ability of their creator.
• They cannot have the Gift.
• A special -2 penalty to size is applied, and the creature cannot have
the Uncommon Size advantage.
Outside of these limitations, each homunculi can be completely different.

Minor Change
Level: 28 Action: Active Type: Spiritual
Effect: Permits altering the appearance of an object or being by modifying
its form. The change is limited to varying its exterior appearance, and does
not alter it’s inherent qualities or nature. In this way, a shining short sword
may become old and rusted, but never changed into a walking stick. In the
case of living things, this spell can change the size of an individual by up
to 2 points, and alter their physical appearance, such as making an ugly
overweight man into a young and good looking one. The spell can be
resisted with a successful MR check by individuals, and a new check may
be repeated once per day. This spell may also affect a number of
individuals or objects, as long as their combined presence is within the
limit determined by the level of the spell.

subject being controlled is not obligated to use any supernatural abilities

natural fighting ability is able to be used by the controller. Resisting the
spell requires a successful MR check. Once under the caster’s control, the
subject has the ability to make a new check once per day, as well as any
time they are ordered to do something entirely against their nature.

Fuse
Level: 42 Action: Active Type: Spiritual
Effect: Unites two beings into a single form, creating a new individual
who possesses the characteristics and abilities of both. The caster
designates which capacities prevail, selecting those they are interested in
from each individual. If the spell joins a fighter and psychic for example,
the resulting individual could have the fighting ability of a warrior, the
psychic powers of the mentalist, and the highest Secondary Abilities of
each. Control of the resulting unified being falls to whichever of the two
wins an opposed Willpower check, although some personality or behaviors
of the losing consciousness may remain. The original bodies of the
individuals remain in their previous state, and as such when the spell
lapses they return to their original state and condition before being fused.
The sum of the presences of both subjects cannot exceed the value
determined by the level of the spell.
It is also possible to merge individuals with things or objects, in which
case the GM may determine the advantages or skills they deem relevant
from such a merge.
Casting Level Base Intermediate Advanced Arcane
Zeon 140 180 240 350
Int. Required 7 9 12 15
Base: 80 MR, 80 Presence Limit
Intermediate: 120 MR, 100 Presence Limit
Advanced: 140 MR, 150 Presence Limit
Arcane: 180 MR, 200 Presence Limit
Maintenance: [15 / 20 / 25 / 30]
Free Access

Spiritual Standstill
Level: 58 Action: Active Type: Spiritual
Effect: As the name of the spell indicates, this preserves the spirit of the
individual on whom it is cast in the same state as the moment it affects
them. From that moment on, the affected person does not make any further
MR or PsR checks, and remains unalterably in the same spiritual
condition. This person cannot be affected by states of any kind, whether
positive or negative. The target may resist the effects of the spell when it is
cast by succeeding at an MR check. Naturally, once under the spell, the
person afflicted may not make another check to free themselves of it’s
effects.

the nearest multiple of 5)
• +20% to any other numeric value present in the spell (with the

Metamorphism
Level: 72 Action: Active Type: Spiritual
Effect: The caster can transform a person or object into anything they wish
(a mouse, a statue, etc.) as long as the Presence of the new form is not
higher than the target’s original amount. Living things metamorphosed into
non-living objects continue to live in their new forms, even if it happens
that they cannot move or breathe. When the spell lapses, the individual or
object returns to their original form. Metamorphism does not permit the
acquisition of any new essential powers or abilities, except for those
possessed by natural creatures with Gnosis 0. Resisting the effects of this
spell requires a successful MR check. If unsuccessful, victims are only
allowed a new check once per day.

Level: 1-80
Recreate
Level: 76 Action: Active Type: Effect
Effect: By manipulating the essence of both body and soul, the caster is
able to restore any damage or loss an individual or object might have
suffered. The caster can decide which parts to recreate, and is not obligated
to restore the subject to its original state. If they use the spell on a one-
eyed, one-armed person, the caster may choose to only recreate the eye, or
only the arm, or both at once. Casting this spell on a wounded individual
or a damaged object makes any damage or penalty disappear automatically
(as long as the caster wishes for it to, of course). Recreate also eliminates
the effect of any supernatural ability that has diminished the individual or
object physically or spiritually; In game terms it can even restore
memories or lost Development Points. The nature of this spell has some
limitations, as follows:
• It only affects losses or damage suffered artificially, not due to the
natural or positive change of the object or individual. In other words,
it cannot ‘restore’ a person or thing to an earlier state that was
damaged or inferior.
• It has no effect on deceased souls, or ones that have passed on into
the flow of souls, and so it cannot restore the dead to life.
• It can only affect a person or item within the presence limit, and in
the special circumstance where the damage was caused by a being
with Gnosis at least 15 points higher than that of the caster, the limit
for presence of this spell is halved.
Casting Level Base Intermediate Advanced Arcane
Zeon 300 500 750 1500
Int. Required 8 12 15 18
17
Base: 60 Presence limit
Intermediate: 120 Presence limit
Advanced: 180 Presence limit
Arcane: 240 Presence limit
Maintenance: None
Create Being
Level: 78 Action: Active Type: Effect
Effect: Creates a magical being with the appearance of life, but completely
under the control of the caster. The entity is developed as a being between
worlds with a Gnosis of 25 as described in Anima: Beyond Fantasy -
Chapter 26: Creation of Beings, and with a number of development
points described by the level of the spell, with the following limitation.
Given that this entity’s existence is directly tied to the soul of the caster,
the number of beings that are created by and that depend on them (whether
through this spell, or the level 82 spells of the minor paths) will limit the
maximum level of the entity. The creature can therefore have at most the
level of it’s creator minus the number of magical entities they have
magically created and are maintaining. For example, if the caster creates
only a single being, it is at most one level lower than it’s creator. This
applies as two levels less if the caster creates two magical beings, etc. If
later, the caster creates more magical beings, the earlier beings
automatically drop one level immediately.

Zeon 250 400 600 1000
Int. Required 9 12 15 17
Base: 600 DP (Level 1)
Intermediate: 1000 DP (Level 5)
Advanced: 1400 DP (Level 9)
Arcane: 1700 DP (Level 12)
Maintenance: [50 / 80 / 120 / 200] Daily
Chimera
Level: 80 Action: Active Type: Effect
Effect: The caster, or their victim, ceases to be a natural being. They are
reformed as a being between worlds with a Gnosis of 25, enjoying all the
advantages and disadvantages that entails. They also obtain the ability to
acquire new essential abilities and powers from the rules in Anima:
Beyond Fantasy - Chapter 26: Creation of Beings, and permits them to
acquire even more development points from disadvantages, up to a limit
based on the level of the spell.
Excess Development Points acquired in this way raise the character level.
For example if they only acquire 170 DP, the character still is considered
to have increased by two levels.
This spell only works on natural creatures, and thus cannot be cast on
beings between worlds or spirits to raise their existing capabilities. All
powers and benefits acquired through this spell depend on the physical
body of the character, and so if by any means they return to their prior
state, or their soul transmigrates into another body or form, they lose the
benefits and advantages of this spell.
Casting Level Base Intermediate Advanced Arcane
Zeon 250 500 1000 2500
Int. Required 10 13 15 17
Base: +100 DP, with up to 100 DP of disadvantages.
Intermediate: +200 DP, with up to 100 DP of disadvantages.
Advanced: +300 DP, with up to 200 DP of disadvantages.
Arcane: +400 DP, with up to 200 DP of disadvantages.
Maintenance: None
High Magic
Zone of Safety
Level: 82 Action: Active Type: Automatic
Effect: Creates a magical zone inside which no person or object can be
harmed in any way by anyone or anything. A person within the zone could
not, for instance, break down a door or even harm a cockroach by stepping
on it by mistake. Zone of Safety automatically affects anyone within it’s
area, including the caster. The affected areas radius is determined by the
level of the spell, and it remains stationary once cast. To overcome the
effect requires a successful MR check, and it is only possible to repeat the
check once per day, even if they leave the zone and then re-enter it.

Provide Soul
Level: 88 Action: Passive Type: Effect
Effect: The caster is able to create a complete soul, giving the breath of
life to an object or body able to contain one. If the soul is provided to a
magically engendered creature such as those developed by the Create
Being spell, or by the Level 82 spells of the minor paths, Provide Soul
makes it unnecessary to continue maintaining those beings with the
caster’s Zeon. The creature thereby breaks any bond it has with its creator,
obtaining free will just like any other being between worlds. Not all souls
created by this spell are capable of entering the body into which they are
introduced. Any soul created this way must be provided a body within the
presence limit described by the level of the spell, and that body cannot
already contain a living soul.

Eternal Magic
Level: 92 Action: Active Type: Effect
Effect: As the name of the spell indicates, it affects another existing spell
and greatly reduces (or even eliminates) the maintenance cost. If the spell
in question has a Daily maintenance cost, Eternal Magic stabilizes it,
making further maintenance unnecessary. The spell continues to be
controlled by it’s caster, but will continue its effect even after their death.
If the spell’s maintenance is instead ‘per combat turn’, it becomes one
requiring Daily maintenance instead.
A spell can only be affected by one Eternal Magic effect at a time, and thus
a ‘per combat turn’ spell cannot be made first Daily, and then Eternal.
Casting Level Base Intermediate Advanced Arcane
Zeon 600 1000 2500 5000
Int. Required 12 14 16 18
Base: Affects a spell of Base level
Intermediate: Affects a spell of Intermediate level
Advanced: Affects a spell of Advanced level
Arcane: Affects a spell of Arcane level
Maintenance: None
Free Access
Level: 1-100
The Barrier
Level: 96 Action: Active Type: Effect
Effect: This unusual spell forms a breach in reality that is capable of
separating the world into two parts. The separation cannot be perceived
naturally, even by those capable of seeing magic. It may adopt any
appearance, from being completely invisible to looking like a simple stone
wall. Whatever form it takes, it does not permit anyone so much as a
glimpse of what lies beyond it. Usually none are aware of its existence,
even when attempting to cross it. If someone attempts to, they exit at
another point of the Barrier, completely unaware of what has transpired (it
is even possible to immediately turn around and not notice it). Transport is
not an automatic spell effect, rather, little by little, reality itself begins to
adapt to the exit location almost imperceptibly. To successfully perceive
the Barrier, an Inhuman (320) Notice check is required. The Barrier can
affect an area or line determined by the level of the spell, and remains in
the same location as it is cast.
As an example, The Barrier can be used over an island, to separate it from
the rest of the natural world. That location disappears from view, and any
ship sailing in the area will see only water, being transported immediately
to another place to innocently continue their voyage. The Barrier need not
be made absolutely perfect. If they choose, the caster may deliberately
leave gaps or openings in one or both directions to successfully cross.
Even for exceptionally powerful beings, certain requirements must be met
to pass through The Barrier. First, they must have a Gnosis of at least 25,
and an exact knowledge of The Barrier’s location. If they meet these
conditions, they may attempt an MR check to bypass the barrier, and may
re-attempt the check once per day if unsuccessful.
Casting Level Base Intermediate Advanced Arcane
Zeon 800 2500 5000 10000
Int. Required 15 16 17 19

Arcane: No size limitations, 300 MR
Maintenance: [40 / 45 / 45 / 50] Daily
The Gift of Life
Level: 98 Action: Active Type: Effect
Effect: The caster is imbued with the ability to create a new form of life,
the first born of a race that has never existed before. The creature can be of
any class (Natural, Being Between Worlds, or Spirit) and can be tied to a
particular element if desired. The maximum level of the creature cannot be
higher than that of the creator. The creator can also choose the Gnosis of
the creation, up to a limit of 10 less than their own.

Create
Level: 100 Action: Active Type: Effect
Effect: Permits the caster absolute dominion over creation, giving them
the ability to create anything they wish: continents, oceans, even entire
worlds. In game terms, the caster receives a pool of Presence to create
whatever they wish, as long as each object is within the presence limit
described by the level of the spell.
This spell can also create new fundamental rules of reality. Altering
gravity, modifying the rate at which time passes, etc. Anyone with a
Gnosis value less than half of the caster’s is affected by such changes, and
there is no resistance to such an effect, it merely is.

fundamental rules.

of the spell.

Increase Weakness
Level: 16 Action: Active Type: Spiritual
Effect: Finds a being’s weak points, and makes them even weaker
supernaturally. In game terms, it doubles the penalties an individual suffers
for an existing weakness or vulnerability. For example, an elemental who
is especially vulnerable to light would suffer quadruple damage, instead of
the standard double damage for such an attack. A character weak to poison
would find their PsnR reduced to one quarter it’s value, instead of the
standard one half for such a disadvantage. To avoid these effects, the target
must pass an MR check, and are only permitted another check when
affected by their weaknesses.

control their summoned creatures. This may be used with equal effect on
either the controller or the controlled creature, but it is always the
controller’s MR that is used for the associated check. If cast upon the
creature, it only affects that creature’s bond, but if cast upon the summoner
directly, it breaks an additional binding for each 10 points by which they
fail the associated MR check. If used against a summoner’s Familiar, he
gains a +40 bonus to the MR check, as the bond is especially deep
between the two souls.

Zone of Weakness
Level: 68 Action: Active Type: Automatic
Effect: The caster weakens the fibers of reality itself within a given area,
making everything inside become fragile and vulnerable. Any damage
produced within the spells area is automatically doubled, and characters
suffer the effects of Critical hits as if their entire body was a vulnerable
point. Structures and constructions automatically lose their Damage
Barrier, and objects with a Fortitude rating such as swords and armor
suffer a -5 penalty to said Fortitude. The spell remains stationary once cast.
Resisting the spell’s effects requires a successful MR check, and a failed
check may only be repeated once per day. However, leaving the area
removes the spells effects immediately.

Devouring Zone
Level: 76 Action: Active Type: Automatic
Effect: Like a deadly vortex, the Devouring Zone spreads, shredding the
essence of everything it finds in its path by slowly consuming it. Little by
little, the things this spell reaches weaken until they are unmade, whether
they are material objects or living beings. Every day the victims (either
people or objects) remain within the area, they must pass a MR or PhR
check, or temporarily have their presence reduced by 5 points. If their
presence falls to 0 from this effect, they decompose, leaving little to no
trace. Living beings also receive an All Action Penalty equivalent to twice
the Presence points lost to this effect. The spiritual damage is treated as if
Sacrificed, and recovers at a rate of 5 points per day once outside the area
of effect.

Void
Level: 90 Action: Active Type: Effect
Effect: Forms a sphere of absolute destruction that absorbs into itself all
physical and spiritual matter around it, unmaking them completely. The
sphere must be located in open space when cast, and remains in a fixed
location until the spell ends. Once cast, it begins suck in anything within
it’s area of influence. The power of attraction of the spell employs an
equivalent force to a Strength 14 to pull in its victims, and anyone failing
an opposed characteristics check is pulled 30m per point of failure towards
the center. The central void is so powerful, that anyone or anything coming
into contact with it weakens until it disappears. Each combat turn they
remain in the central void’s radius, they must succeed at two resistance
checks. The first, an MR check, where failure will reduce the victim’s
Zeon equal to the level of failure. The second is a PhR check, where
failure will reduce the victim’s Life points equal to the level of failure. If
the character is completely drained of Zeon, they begin to permanently
lose a point of Power for every 100 points of Zeon they would lose. If their
power falls to 0 this way, or if they die from loss of Life Points, their being
is totally absorbed by the nothingness. Although the caster is not affected
by the attracting power of the void, he will suffer it’s consequences if
placed in it’s nucleus like anyone else.

Chaos
Level: 98 Action: Active Type: Automatic
Effect: A spell of almost insuperable power, since it is capable of shaking
even the very pillars of reality. Upon being unleashed, the caster causes the
very order of things to crumble, altering the balance of all things in a
completely unexpected way. Inside this zone of chaos, nothing works as it
should, and all events are unnaturally twisted. For example, Chaos can
affect gravity, making some things light as feathers and others as heavy as
lead. It can affect the weather, causing icy winds only yards from a tropical
gale or heat wave. This spell does not affect only the natural elements,
though. It can influence the feelings and instincts of living beings, making
lovers hate each other with a passion, or irreconcilable enemies capable of
bonds of peace. This spell can even affect the passage of time, making it
flow more quickly or slowly (this said, it cannot allow you to travel to the
past). This is all to say, Chaos is capable of affecting practically any
imaginable facet of reality.
The caster can designate specific aspects of reality they wish to alter, or on
the other hand, transform all of reality completely. In either case, this spell
does not give the caster direct control of the changes, so the results and
consequences can be as the name of the spell implies, completely chaotic.
The spell has a radius based on the level of the spell, and is stationary once
cast. Additionally, especially powerful beings have the ability to resist this
change, depending on the level of the spell, as their spiritual and existential
force is great enough to maintain order.
Casting Level Base Intermediate Advanced Arcane
Zeon 700 1200 2000 5000
Int. Required 14 16 18 20
25
Base: Affects Gnosis 10 beings, 100 km Radius
Intermediate: Affects Gnosis 20 beings, 1000 km Radius
Advanced: Affects Gnosis 30 beings, 10000 km Radius
Arcane: Affects Gnosis 40 beings, no distance limitations.
Maintenance: [70 / 80 / 90 / 100] Daily
Uncreation
Level: 100 Action: Active Type: Automatic
Effect: This spell permits the caster to designate an aspect of existence,
one as far reaching as a city, or a race of peoples, or as precise as a single
individual, and simply make it cease to exist. The unmade item, or person,
is erased from reality with all the consequences this implies, so that it had
never existed. No one who knew of what was unmade can remember it,
and all events it may have affected are modified as though what was
unmade had no hand in it; even returning to life those whose deaths the
unmade may have caused (as long as the victim’s souls remain). Only
entities with a Gnosis of 40, or those who have ascended with a value 20
higher than their Natura will remember the unmade, due to their superior
existential force. To avoid the effects of this spell, even if it targets a
multitude of people or objects, the highest value among them is used to
perform a MR check. It is only possible to unmake those things that exist
in the present, and not something purely in the past.

of the blow’s strength, and uses the Impact attack type.

Absurd (180) difficulty, or have the ability to otherwise see magic, can
perceive of the attack at all.

Whirlwind
Level: 50 Action: Active Type: Automatic

determined by the level of the spell. It leaves nothing but a trail of
destruction behind it, and any subject within the area will automatically

failure, the victims are sucked into the air within the whirlwind, and suffer

100 and 130 meters.
The only condition for being affected is being within the area after the first
round where the whirlwind is created, but does not affect immaterial
bodies or those not affected by air.
The caster may propel the whirlwind as desired with a movement value of

Hurricane
Level: 72 Action: Active Type: Automatic
Effect: Creates a tremendous gale that sweeps away everything within a
large area. All characters inside the area must succeed an opposed
characteristics check against Strength 12, or be taken by the wind. All
constructions within the area with a damage barrier lower than 60 is
destroyed automatically, while those with damage barriers below 120
suffer 10 points of damage each turn until they are destroyed utterly.
Especially sturdy structures with damage barriers above 120 remain
unaffected by the magical hurricane.
Bodies dragged by the wind remain suspended in the air until the spell
dissipates, at which point they plunge to the ground. Distance to the
ground can vary according to both the environment and the casters’
desires, but at no point may it exceed 300 meters. Characters who succeed
at the opposed check are safe and make no further checks as long as they
remain immobile. The GM may decide to apply a bonus to the affected
characters’ checks depending on their circumstances.

Solid Air
Level: 76 Action: Active Type: Effect, Attack
Effect: Solidifies the very air, producing resistant, compact matter. The
magician may choose its form and location, but the area of the solidified
air is limited to an area based on the level of the spell. Possible uses of this
spell are to obstruct an entrance with a solid block of air, or building an
invisible bridge to cover a gap, for example. Only attacks capable of
damaging energy can affect the solid air, and it is counted as having 150
Life Points per 15m before breaking. The only way this substance can be
seen is by the viewer passing a Notice check of Inhuman (320), or a
Search check of Absurd (180).
29
An additional use for this spell is to fence people in, preventing them from
moving freely. When used with this purpose, attacks may be performed
with the rules for trapping; no penalties apply to the magician’s projection
ability for this purpose, and the spell is considered to have a Strength value
of 14 for opposed checks.

Liquid Control
Level: 22 Action: Active Type: Effect, Spiritual
Effect: Grants the caster complete control over an amount of liquid mass
in the nearby area. The caster may alter the physical characteristics of this
fluid (such as color and clarity) as well as it’s solidity. The liquid can even
be moved without contact. When used against an elemental based on
water, the caster can attempt to control the creature unless it succeeds at an
MR check. Creatures that fail this check may attempt a new check each
day. In Addition, this spell can also manipulate the bloodstreams of living
organisms. However, in these cases the victims may attempt either an MR
or PhR check, and they receive a +40 bonus. This style of manipulation
allows the caster to apply an all action penalty, as well as damage per
combat turn, equal to half the level of failure for the check. Victims who
fail the check for this style of manipulation are allowed a new check every
5 combat turns.

Liquid Body
Level: 52 Action: Active Type: Effect
Effect: Transforms a designated body into a liquid substance, rendering it
immune to many types of attacks and able to shift it’s form at the caster’s
will. Because of this, the Liquid Body can transform it’s appendages into
natural weapons, with a +5 quality bonus. This limited metamorphism is
very effective, and so anyone observing them must pass a Notice check of
Very Difficult to perceive it’s liquid nature.
Although not strictly an immaterial being, liquefied bodies are able to pass
through any opening or hole that water can. While in this state, those
affected are completely invulnerable to any attack of the Thrust and Cut
attack types while those based on Impact will have half of their regular
damage. If an attack is capable of damaging energy, it acts normally
against a liquefied character.
Characters in this state are vulnerable to the paralyzing cold, and related
effects, suffering a resistance penalty of -20 to checks against them.

Reflect States
Level: 56 Action: Passive Type: Automatic

the same suffering. The reflected state remains for as long as the original
target continues to suffer the effects, or until this spell is dissipated. Each
effect can only be reflected (or attempted to be reflected) once.

the spell. All creatures inside the area of effect after the first round it is cast

Soul Reflection
Level: 76 Action: Active Type: Effect, Attack
Effect: Produces a spiritual reflection of a person or creature. The result is
an animistic copy of the individual, featuring each and every quality
present in the original. In game terms, the entity is identical to the original

interact with the real world, and visible to all (unless the original is also
invisible), it is immune to attacks not capable of damaging energy. Powers
of the original creature that require an elevated Gnosis above 20 cannot be
duplicated.
The reflection will obey the caster’s orders, but it can only materialize in
the presence of the subject upon whose likeness it reflects. It can also only
target beings within the level limit set by the level of the spell. Unwilling
targets can attempt a MR check to avoid being copied, and the caster
cannot target themselves.

Effect: Freezes magic itself, temporarily stopping spells and canceling
their effects. Spells do not dissipate while they are stopped. However, they
are suspended and unable to affect anything, and they also do not require
maintenance while in this state. Freeze magic affects spells both from the
caster and from other characters, provided their combined Zeon is within
the limit set by the level of the spell. The suspended spells will regain
activity as soon as Freeze Magic dissipates. As this is a passive spell, it can
even be used to stop spells activated within the same turn it is cast.

Fire Mine
Level: 40 Action: Active Type: Effect
Effect: Creates an igneous mine ready to explode in a radius around a
designated point. The explosion is considered a Heat attack, with base
damage set by the level of the spell. The extent of the damage is also set by
the proximity of the target to the center of the mine upon it’s activation.
• If the distance between the character and the mine is more than half
of the explosion area, they must defend against an automatic attack
of Almost Impossible difficulty (a final attack of 240).
• If the distance between the character and the mine is less than half
the explosion area, they must defend against an automatic attack of
Impossible difficulty (a final attack of 280)
• If the character is less than five meters from the source of the mine,
they must defend against an automatic attack of Inhuman difficulty
(a final attack of 320)
The mine is placed at a particular place or object when cast, and can be
activated as an active action. It is not possible to select targets inside the
explosion area, including the caster themselves.

Vital Sacrifice
Level: 56 Action: Active Type: Effect
Effect: Allows the caster to consume their own life force to increase their
physical abilities. This spell grants the passive ability to temporarily
sacrifice life points up to a limit each combat turn to obtain a bonus for a
given action. Every 5 life points sacrificed this way will provide the
character +5 to one of their rolls (in the case of damage resistant creatures,
multiply the life point cost by their life point multiple). This bonus will
only be applicable to physical actions, but can be added to primary abilities
such as attack, defense or Magic Projection. Despite being a passive
action, it is necessary to declare the amount sacrificed before any dice are
rolled. It is also possible that the caster decides to confer this ability on
another individual, then it is the target who decides how many points to
spend in each case. Life points lost in this way are recovered at a rate of 10
a day, no matter what the natural Regeneration of the character indicates
(that is, they count as a Sacrifice). This spell’s effects do not overlap, and
only one sacrifice spell may be active on an individual at a time.

Magnetic Control
Level: 46 Action: Active Type: Effect
Effect: Grants the caster control over all magnetic fields within a radius
around them. In that area, it allows the caster to dictate the movement of
any metallic body with a force equivalent to an independent Strength
value, set by the level of the spell.
The control over magnetism is such that any action is considered an
Automatic effect upon the metal. This allows, for example, the full
paralysis of an individual fully in metal armor with no associated MR
check, or to snatch a sword directly from an opponent’s hands, requiring
no Magic Projection to do so. Alternatively, characters can overcome these
effects by opposing the Strength of the spell with either their own Strength,
or Agility. If used to make an attack with a metal weapon at a distance, use
the rules for the Psychic power Minor Psychokinesis and the caster’s
Magic projection.
This act of control is an active action, and thus the caster must have the
active combat initiative to execute it (and thus cannot use it as a defense).
If a body is composed of metal only in part (such as an axe head with a
weighty shaft of wood), or is shielded with energy barriers, the Strength
value of the spell receives a -4 penalty.

character from using other defensive abilities just as a damage resistance
creature. These bonus life points from this spell are subtracted before the
character’s own when suffering any damage.

Petrify
Level: 60 Action: Active Type: Spiritual
Effect: Transforms the target into a stone statue. The victim cannot move
and has no awareness of the events going on around them for the duration
of the spell. This can be maintained for years, but the affected character
does not age. As soon as the spell dissipates, the individual returns to their
original condition. Any damage or breakage to the statue can result in
injury, or even death, to the actual character. The target of the spell can
resist it’s effects by succeeding at an MR check. Upon failure, the subject
is allowed a new check the following day for the first attempt, and then
any subsequent attempt at weekly intervals.

Fissure
Level: 62 Action: Active Type: Effect
Effect: Causes a violent, but highly concentrated tremor that splits the
earth, creating a fissure. The dimensions of the fissure’s maximum length
and width are determined by the level of the spell, and can be between 20
to 50 meters deep. Individuals in the area of the fissure forming must pass
an Agility check to avoid falling into the fissure and suffering the
appropriate impact damage. Constructions in the area can also be severely
damaged, as long as their damage barrier is sufficiently low. If they are
above the limit set by the level of the spell, their structure is too dense or
sturdy to be trapped or damaged by the fissure.

Meteor
Level: 90 Action: Active Type: Attack
Effect: Allows the caster to control the trajectory of one or more meteors
and throw them from the heavens to cause unprecedented devastation.
Despite being an attack spell, the arrival of each meteor requires between
one and ten rounds after the cast to arrive, determined by rolling 1d10 to
determine it’s arrival in combat turns after the spell is cast.
Each meteor produces an attack with a base damage of 200 to the impact
site and 10 meters around it, using either the Heat or Impact attack type.
Everything within 50 meters of the central impact will suffer the effects of
the blast wave, which is an attack with 60 base damage and an impact with
a Strength score of 14. Naturally, the spell cannot produce it’s full effects if
cast indoors or underground, so it’s consequences can be altered depending
on the environment.

Atomic Control
Level: 100 Action: Active Type: Automatic
Effect: The caster is able to mold the atomic matter of their surroundings
like clay. To them, everything that moves is nothing but a set of atoms to
manipulate as they desire. They gain full dominion over all organic and
inorganic matter within the area.
There is no limit to what the caster can do to with a body or object under
their control; they may alter it’s shape and appearance, transform it into
something else, or simply scatter its atoms, thereby causing it’s utter
destruction. Since this spell controls physical matter, it has no effect on
souls or completely immaterial bodies. Those wishing to avoid the effects
must immediately succeed at an MR or PhR check, or they are forever
subject to the control of the caster.

Acquire Natural Capacities
Level: 36 Action: Active Type: Effect
Effect: Grants the caster or those designated by them the ability to acquire
abilities and characteristics of natural beings, such as plants or animals. In
game terms, it gives the target a number of additional development points
to acquire secondary abilities, essential abilities, and powers described in
Anima: Beyond Fantasy - Chapter 26: Creation of Beings.
Of secondary abilities, only ones based on Strength, Agility, or Perception
can be acquired. Of powers, only those with a Gnosis requirement of 0 can
be acquired. Otherwise, the DP is spent according to the current class of
the character, and cannot be used to increase primary abilities or acquire
supernatural powers.
This spell can temporarily alter the appearance of the character, but not
their state as a natural creature. For instance, a character acquiring an
increased natural damage may sprout horns or claws.
A character can only be affected by one application of this spell at a time,
and to acquire new powers, the existing spell must be dismissed, and a new
spell cast.

Life Mind
Level: 42 Action: Active Type: Automatic
Effect: Connects the caster’s essence with any living organism in a large
area, as long as their Gnosis is 10 or lower, including natural plants and
animals. The caster is able to feel what is around those beings using their
senses as if they were the caster’s own, but does not allow for control of
those creatures. Since this also transmits part of the caster’s spirit, they can
cast spells from the creature with half of their Magic Accumulation as if
they were physically present. However, this also means that any Spiritual
spell cast upon the creature in which the caster finds themselves will also
affect the caster. Usually, an affected individual does not realize they are
being possessed in this way, even if spells are cast from their body.
The instant the caster tries to introduce their essence to the target, a
successful MR check by the target will avoid the effects entirely. Once the
caster is observing from that body, they are free to ‘jump’ to any other
body within range of the spell (as long as that target also fails their
resistance roll). Once an individual resists successfully, the caster can no
longer affect them, as if they left the area of effect.
As this spell is not a Detection spell, it does not allow the caster to know
how many valid targets are around them or their exact location, but the
caster can direct their power to an area where they are interested in
directing their spirit. It is possible, for example, to decide that the caster
wants to enter the nearest mind to the point 300 meters to the north, and
the spell will search for such a being. However, this method can be of
limited use, since it is quite possible that the nearest living organism would
be nothing more than a blade of grass.
While a part of the casters’ essence resides outside their original body, they
are unaware of the events taking place around their true body’s
surroundings.

Shield Area
Level: 66 Action: Active Type: Automatic
Effect: Enchants a certain area, making it impenetrable for a certain type
of being. At the time of casting the spell, the caster must choose which
kind of beings will be forbidden, although they will have absolute freedom
to select as many as desired. It could, for example, prohibit the passage
only to fire elementals or allow only humans to enter.
The affected area may not be longer in radius than the spell's degree
indicates, or double in length if it is instead wall-shaped. That is to say, if
cast at base degree, the caster can choose between the area having a radius
of 20 meters or a length of 40 meters in line.
Any being who has been forbidden and who does not succeed at an MR
check will not be able to cross the shield. Anyone who has failed the
resistance roll has the right to repeat the roll once an hour. If any of these
beings were already inside a shielded area when the spell was cast, they
can move freely until they leave the area and intend to re-enter. Shield
Area remains static in the place where it is cast.

of the target, and not the mind. Those who fail the roll are entitled to a new
resistance roll every day, as well as any time it receives an order that
directly opposes it’s nature.

Share Essence
Level: 72 Action: Active Type: Effect
Effect: The caster ties the essence of two individuals together in an
indivisible unity, so long as their combined presence is within the limit set
by the level of the spell. In this way, all damage and Spiritual effects
suffered by one equally affects the other. Both individuals use the highest
MR of the two against spiritual spells. When the essence of a damage
resistant creature is combined with a normal being, the latter only suffers
one fifth of the damage from the former. Avoiding these effects requires a
successful MR check.

Free Access
Level: 1-80
Transmigrate Soul
Level: 76 Action: Active Type: Spiritual
Effect: Confers upon the caster the ability to transport any soul in their
presence to a new host. The soul can be hosted in a living being, or an
inanimate object such as a stone, or sword. A dead body, in good condition,
can also serve as a host, in which case the body will return to life. The
transmigrated soul adopts the physical capabilities and limitations of the
new host, retaining only their intellectual and spiritual abilities. For
example, if the new host is a tree, the soul will be able to think, but unable
to speak or move, or see with any capacity not innate to their body. Once
the transmigration is complete, the soul will remain in it’s new host until it
is destroyed.
If the soul transmigrates to a body, or object already in possession of a
soul, it will naturally face resistance. The two souls engage in a struggle
for control of the host, and each roll 1d100 and add their base presence (no
open rolls are allowed for this contested check). The results for the winner
depend on the margin of victory.
• 100+: The victor consumes the other soul and takes control of the
host permanently.
• 50-99: The weaker soul is pacified for a time, and remains asleep
until a significant event awakens it.
• 0-50: Temporary control is established, but the weaker soul is awake
and entitled to a new check once per day.
This spell can be resisted by both the transmigrated soul and the soul of a
host, requiring a successful MR check.
This spell can affect deceased spirits still awaiting the call, but cannot
bring someone back from the flow of souls. If a supernatural spirit is
enclosed in a physical body, it may lose it’s mystic powers, and reduce it’s
level if necessary.
Casting Level Base Intermediate Advanced Arcane
Zeon 180 240 300 540
Int. Required 9 12 15 18
Base: 60 Presence limit, 100 MR
Intermediate: 100 Presence limit, 140 MR
Advanced: 140 Presence limit, 160 MR
Arcane: 180 Presence limit, 200 MR

Spiritual Existence
Level: 80 Action: Active Type: Effect
Effect: Allows the caster to transcend to a spiritual state, definitively
abandoning their physical body. From that moment, they will cease to be a
natural creature or being between worlds and will become a spirit with
Gnosis 25, enjoying all the advantages (and disadvantages, such as being
affected by summoning) that this entails. Additionally, they will have the
ability to obtain extra Development Points, which can be used to choose
between Essential Abilities and Powers described in Anima: Beyond
Fantasy - Chapter 26: Creation of Beings. It also allows you to buy
disadvantages and penalties to obtain additional points.
Keep in mind that excess development points increases the level of the
caster; When using this spell, the sorcerer automatically gains one level
(by becoming a spiritual entity) plus an additional one for every 100 DP he
obtains without counting disadvantages.
This spell only works on natural beings and beings between worlds, so it
cannot be cast on spirits to increase their abilities again. In the event that a
spiritual being is reincarnated again in a physical form, they will
automatically lose the advantages and powers obtained through this spell.

Zeon 250 500 1000 2500
Int. Required 10 13 15 17

Intermediate: +100 DP, 100 DP from disadvantages
Advanced: +200 DP, 200 DP from disadvantages
Arcane: +300 DP, 300 DP from disadvantages
Maintenance: None

Tie Vital Essence
Level: 86 Action: Active Type: Spiritual
Effect: Hides a soul in a physical container, without completely detaching
it from its original form. The affected person will continue to act through
their old body with freedom, since there is still a link with the soul,
although this will be nothing more than an immortal empty shell, since his
vital essence is safe elsewhere. However, the fact that they do not die does
not mean that they are invulnerable. The body is not immune to criticals
and they may be incapacitated completely. In addition, although they are
unable to perish because of the loss of life points, if these are reduced such
or if they suffer from the state of death, their body becomes a lifeless shell
that will only heal with supernatural abilities. The carcass without a soul
will be immune to the mystical effects of a spiritual nature, that is, any
effect that directly affects its essence. On the contrary, the container itself
is vulnerable, and if it is destroyed the character will die automatically.
Since there is still a link between the body and the object that serves as a
hiding place for the soul, they cannot be separated at a great distance at the
risk of breaking the union and the individual dies. This distance is
determined by the degree of the spell. If someone wants to resist this
effect, they must succeed at an MR check, but once failed, they will not
have the right to repeat it until they are in physical contact with the
container.

Level: 1-100
Resurrection
Level: 96 Action: Active Type: Effect
Effect: Plucks from the flow of souls a person’s very essence, and returns
it to life. The difficulty of the spell depends on the time that has elapsed
from the moment in which the person died, since the longer this period, the
more the spirit is dispersed and rooted within the flow of souls. As such,
the individual must be within both the presence and time limits of the
degree of the spell.
This spell only allows you to bring back the soul of the individual, but you
cannot tie it to your body alone, nor heal it directly. To make the
resurrection completely effective, the spirit must be transferred to an
appropriate body, through the spell Transmigrate Soul. There is the
possibility that their time in the afterlife has irreparably affected the soul of
the individual, so it is up to the game master to determine the limit and
knowledge of the individual.
Obviously, it is not possible to resurrect the soul of one who has already
been reincarnated, or had their soul destroyed.

Lord of Souls
Level: 100 Action: Active Type: Automatic
Effect: The caster seizes control of a portion of the flow of souls itself,
giving him absolute dominion over all souls, Spirits, and similar beings in
a wide radius. Among other uses, the caster can snatch a soul from one
body and reintroduce it into an unborn child, thus producing Nephalim.
The lord of souls conveys their orders to those under their dominion
through a mystical connection that does not require physical proximity nor
language. Those wishing to resist these effects must succeed at an MR
check, and those who fail this check are entitled to a new check each day,
or any time they are given an order completely against their nature. If the
caster is attempting to separate the soul from it’s original body, the target
receives a special +40 to their resistance roll.
Naturally, this spell only affects creatures with souls, and so it does not
affect the undead or inanimate constructs.

Create Illusory Being
Level: 40 Action: Active Type: Automatic
Effect: Creates an illusory being. The entity is fashioned according to the
caster’s desires, using the rules described in Anima: Beyond Fantasy –
Chapter 26: Creation of Beings. This is a being between worlds,
however, its illusory nature automatically grants it the Physical
Exemption ability. This creature can be at most 2 levels above the level of
the caster. Because the creature is not real, it can not inflict damage or
affect physical reality whatsoever. All attacks incapable of damaging
energy pass right through it, causing no damage in any way.
The spell must be cast upon a specific area with a given radius, and all
those within must succeed at an MR check to disbelieve the creature’s
existence. Even though the spell is circumscribed to a specific area, the
creature may chase after someone outside the range of the spell, however it
remains unseen to any not previously within the spell’s area.
Just as characters who succeed at the Mr check do not perceive the
creature, it likewise cannot perceive those who succeed to disbelieve it,
and will ignore them.

Distort Detection
Level: 52 Action: Active Type: Automatic
Effect: Grants the caster the ability to alter the result of any supernatural
detection around them. They can falsify the information in any way they
desire, increasing or decreasing it’s potential, its abilities, or location of
detection. The character that casts the detection ability must succeed at an
MR check to avoid being deceived. The spell does not work directly
against individuals within the area of effect, but against the detection
method itself. Therefore, if someone tries to detect something that is within
the radius of the spell, they must also succeed at the MR check or be
affected. It would, for example, affect a sorcerer who from miles away
tries to magically locate the caster or someone next to them. For this same
reason, by directly affecting the means of detection, the person who uses it
does not realize that they had to overcome the MR check to avoid being
deceived.

Level: 1-90
Illusion of the Senses
Level: 86 Action: Active Type: Spiritual
Effect: Allows the caster to lead a subject to believe whatever the caster
wishes. Though the caster does not actually modify reality, the spell
convinces the affected character that an illusion is so real, they will act as
if it is so in every aspect. For example, if the caster wishes to trick
someone into thinking they have lost all of their arms and legs, the
character will lose all feeling in those limbs and fall to the ground in the
belief that they have in fact, lost them.
Another example of this could be a fighter suffering from wounds who is
led to believe that not only are they in perfect shape, but also that they
possess a +50 all action bonus (which is of course, fictional). A character
convinced they are dead falls immediately into unconsciousness.
If the associated MR or PsR is failed, they receive a new check only upon
being convinced they are being deceived.

Non-Existence
Level: 90 Action: Active Type: Automatic
Effect: The target of this spell cannot be perceived by any of the natural
senses – although they still materially exist. Others cannot see, hear, smell,
touch, or taste the presence of the individual in any way. In addition, the
target does not leave any visible trace or marks behind while the spell is
maintained. The only way to realize a non-existent character’s presence is
via supernatural means (such as magic, ki, or psychic detections).
A character can resist the effects of the spell by passing an MR check.
Those who fail are entitled to repeat it every time they have reason to
believe there is another being around.

Deceive Death
Level: 92 Action: Active Type: Effect
Effect: The caster manages to deceive death itself. In game terms, while
active, the individual will not die in any way, as the flow of souls ignores
their presence and will not claim them.
Although the spirit will not leave the body, the physical form can be
shattered, and the character continues to suffer the negative effects of such
damage. This spell only affects characters within a certain level limit.
This spell does not protect from an effect that completely destroys the soul
of the individual.

World of Lies
Level: 96 Action: Active Type: Automatic
Effect: Creates an artificial reality that the caster can modify as they
please. They might create a city out of nowhere and turn it into a fantasy
land all in the next combat turn, for example. Imagination is the only limit.
This spell covers a large radius within which all illusions apply the Ghostly
spell rules. Everyone entering the spell’s area must succeed at an MR
check or fall beneath the power of this spell. World of Lies also allows the
caster to create ghostly creatures to populate it, and is given a number of
levels to distribute for this purpose – although none of them can have more
than half the level of the caster, rounded up. For example, a level 10 caster
can create 100 level 1 beings, or 20 of level 5, or any combination
between. These beings are created as beings between worlds, using the
rules described in Anima: Beyond Fantasy – Chapter 26: Creation of
Beings. These creatures have illusory intelligence and life, and are able to
act independently or follow the orders of the caster.
Anyone who fails the resistance check can only repeat it when
encountering something causing them to doubt the reality of their
surroundings.
Casting Level Base Intermediate Advanced Arcane
Zeon 500 900 1400 2000
Int. Required 13 15 17 19
Base: 140 MR, 1km Radius, 100 Levels
Intermediate: 180 MR, 100km Radius, 500 Levels
Advanced: 220 MR, 1000km Radius, 1500 Levels
Arcane: 260 MR, 10000km Radius, 5000 Levels
Maintenance: [50 / 90 / 140 / 200] Daily
Free Access
Level: 1-100
False Reality
Level: 100 Action: Active Type: Automatic
Effect: This spell creates a distorted reality, and makes the false real. In
this way, events that can never have taken place can be invented and made
to produce effects upon the present. History is not truly altered, since the
spell only manages to change the state of things at the time it is cast,
however few are able to tell the difference. The spell cannot accomplish
the impossible; it can only alter feasible events which are likely to have
happened if the appropriate situation is described by the caster.
For example, the caster may say a certain mountain pass has been blocked
due to a landslide caused by thieves making a daring escape. According to
this set of events, the False Reality spell would create a random group of
burglars and all individuals in the pass at the time. As another example,
the caster could claim that a non-existent entity called Ethon is about to
destroy a city. They only need justify this by saying it was born by magic
of a great sorcerer in the past – it is all up to the creativity they put into the
lie.
Calculating whom needs to attempt an MR check for this spell is difficult
to do; the being or object within the lie that has the highest resistance or
those affected by it is considered. In the first example above, it seems
natural that the landslide rocks or the thieves themselves need pass the
check. However, if there is a high level individual in the designated pass at
the time, he could use his own resistance value instead of those of the
thieves. In the “Ethon” example, the most logical approach would be for
the GM to find a suitable sorcerer to create such a being, and use their
resistance. Keep in mind, that the greater event described, the more likely
powerful entities are related to the event.
Since this spell does not directly change history, it is not possible to bring
the dead back to life, nor make someone die within the distant past. The
reason for this is that it is not feasible to return someone from a time or
place where the flow of souls has claimed them. However, if an event is
described that implies a change preventing someone’s death or returning
them to life, they would do so without a soul, as an empty shell of what
they really were.
This spell additionally does not increase or decrease the abilities or
characteristics of any characters involved. As well, any being with Gnosis
superior to the caster need not attempt a check to disbelieve the story.

Zeon 600 1000 2000 3000
Int. Required 14 16 18 19
