Advanced: 220 MR
Arcane: 240 MR
Maintenance: None

Effect: Allows a living person to defeat death. In game terms, the target

Beings. If the caster chooses for the target to become a Spirit, the target
must spend 100 of the Development Points gained to that end.

described in the spell Chimera in the path of creation.

already of a necromantic nature.

allows them to absorb half of all lost life points of living beings within a
radius around them. These life points go toward healing any sort of wound
they have incurred. Well of Life itself does not inflict any damage, but
typically casters incorporate this spell with other damaging magics at their
disposal.
Casting Level Base Intermediate Advanced Arcane
Zeon 300 400 500 600
Int. Required 10 12 14 16
Base: 50m Radius
Intermediate: 250m Radius
Advanced: 500m Radius
Arcane: 1km Radius
Maintenance: [15 / 20 / 25 / 30]
Cursed Land
Level: 88 Action: Active Type: Effect
Effect: By corrupting a small fragment of the world, the caster enchants an
area of land, causing any being that dies within to rise instantly as an
undead under their control. The deceased automatically become animated
corpses, although powerful beings with Gnosis at least 5 points above their
Natura, rise as a Specter instead (as if it had been affected by the spell
Raise Specter).
Creatures can only act while inside of the cursed land. If they leave it’s
area, the magic that sustains them disappears, and they die.
Once cast, the area affected remains stationary.

Sustenance
Level: 90 Action: Active Type: Effect
Effect: Affects the essence of one or several undead maintained by the
caster, allowing for their existence after the caster stops maintaining their
existence. For example, if the caster uses Sustenance upon an animated
corpse from the spell Raise Corpses, the creature will continue to ‘live’
once the spell has ended. The caster may affect as many undead as desired,
as long as their total presence is within the limit set by the spell.

Raw Material

corpses and spirits, enabling the caster to create the raw material necessary
to resurrect as corpses at a later stage. The spell forms the necromantic
materials, equivalent to a number of human bodies based on the level of
the spell. In the case of other, more powerful creatures, a smaller number is
used instead based on the GM’s discretion.

area must succeed at an MR check or be subject to the complete control of
the caster. If failed, no additional attempts to escape are possible, unless

Come Back From The Dead
Level: 98 Action: Active Type: Effect
Effect: Returns a deceased creature’s spirit to the world, even after it has
returned to the flow of souls or if their essence has been dispersed. The
creature returns as an undead being, but keeps their soul and essence
intact. If the body of the deceased still exists, it returns to it. If it does not
exist, they may return to a new one provided or become an immaterial
specter. As is the case in similar spells, the level of the entity and the time
since their death are limiting factors for this spell. Souls that have been
utterly destroyed or since resurrected after death can not be affected.
Casting Level Base Intermediate Advanced Arcane
Zeon 400 800 1600 3200
Int. Required 16 17 18 19

Intermediate: One year from death, Level 8
Advanced: Ten years from death, Level 10
Arcane: One Century from death, Level 16
Maintenance: None
The Awakening
Level: 100 Action: Active Type: Automatic
Effect: The most powerful of the necromantic spells. By corrupting a part
of the soul of the world, the caster extends their shadow throughout
existence, returning life to the dead and submitting them to the caster’s
dominion. The awakening does not have a certain range of action; it
equally affects any deceased whose level was not higher than described by
the level of the spell. If their spirit still awaits the call, they rise as specters,
otherwise as animated corpses. Those beings who have a Gnosis 15 points
above their Natura, return as undead with all of their faculties and abilities.

Tie
Level: 1-10 Action: Active Type: Effect
Effect: This spell causes chains, ropes, or any type of string to be tied with
an independent Sleight of Hand ability. Alternately, the caster may
command ropes to tie up a subject, in which case the target must defend
against an automatic trapping attack of the same difficulty as the Sleight of
Hand value. The Strength value of the binding once struck depends on the
material used. For example, thick rope would have a Strength value of 10,
and metallic chains a value of 12. Rope and other bindings are not directly
created by the spell.

Effect: Increase or decrease the size of the person or object the spell is cast
upon. Regardless of the spell level used, the size of the target can not be
reduced below 1. Resisting the effects requires a successful MR check.

Change of Outlook
Level: 30-40 Action: Active Type: Spiritual
Effect: Modifies the facial and bodily appearance of the subject on which
it is cast. The caster can alter the victim’s skin color or physiognomy, but
does not vary the height or weight of the victim beyond the limits of their
size value. To resist these effects, it is necessary to succeed at an MR
check. Once failed, it is only possible to repeat the check once a day.

Extend Presence
Level: 30-40 Action: Active Type: Effect
Effect: The caster can separate their soul from their body at will, allowing
them to act remotely as if they were truly there. While the caster is
separated, they will not be able to move, but will be able to make their soul
return to their body whenever desired without having to end the spell.
The expanded presence can touch physical objects and even attack, but it
will be invisible to the human eye; It is only possible to notice it through
the use of Ki detection, the ability to see spirits, or some other means of
seeing ethereal bodies (although if their presence is holding something,
that object is perfectly visible).
The presence is intangible and can only be damaged by attacks capable of
damaging energy. Any loss of life points will also affect the caster’s body.
The maximum distance that the presence can separate from the body is
determined by the level of the spell.
Casting Level Base Intermediate Advanced Arcane
Zeon 100 150 200 250
Int. Required 7 10 12 15
Base: 5m Radius
Intermediate: 25m Radius
Advanced: 50m Radius
Arcane: 100m Radius, also creates copies of objects brought by the caster
allowing their expanded presence to use them.
Maintenance: [10 / 15 / 20 / 25] Daily
Closed Paths: None

Purification
Level: 30-40 Action: Active Type: Effect
Effect: Purify a target by eliminating any harmful element, such as poisons
and toxins. Purification only affects harmful elements of a body that do not
belong to it. In this way, the poison that a snake possesses naturally will
not be altered, since it is something natural for that creature, but it will be
eliminated if inoculated into a bite victim.

True Close
Level: 30-40 Action: Active Type: Effect
Effect: Through this spell, the caster automatically obstructs any type of
door, shutter, or window. If for example, it is cast upon an open door, it
will close by itself. If upon the latch, it will physically bar the exit. While
the spell is maintained, it is not possible to open the door physically, and
only mystical effects or destruction will open it. The maximum presence of
the opening affected is determined by the level of the spell.

Absorb Information
Level: 40-50 Action: Active Type: Effect
Effect: The caster absorbs written or visual knowledge within sight or
reach. Thus, it is possible for example, to read a book in a few seconds by
touching its cover and remember the details as if it had been perused
carefully. A character who tries to remember information memorized using
this spell decreases the difficulty of any Memorize check by 2 degrees.

Curse
Level: 40-50 Action: Active Type: Spiritual
Effect: Causes a series of negative conditions on the person it affects. It
causes misfortune when certain actions are performed by the victim:
maintaining a romantic relationship, writing poetry, helping someone…
The caster decides what exactly the action is, as well as it’s consequences,
within the limit set by the level of the spell.
By it’s nature, the spell does not influence only those directly targeted, but
also others who interact with them if they enter the designation of the
curse. In this case, every individual is entitled to their own MR check. For
example, a young girl who is interested in forming a relationship with a
boy affected by a curse that prevents others from falling in love with him,
would feel discouraged from this action should she fail the MR of the
spell. New resistance checks may only be performed when the condition is
met an additional time.
Casting Level Base Intermediate Advanced Arcane
Zeon 200 350 500 700
Int. Required 8 10 13 16
Base: 120 MR, Only affects very specific acts (such as falling in love with
a certain person, or writing a poem for the king) and only produces the
inability to achieve such goals with complete success.
Intermediate: 140 MR, Affects generic acts (falling in love, fighting,
helping others…) produces true misfortune, where the target cannot
achieve the goal; or if cursing a primary or secondary ability, a -60 penalty
every time it is used.
Advanced: 160 MR, Affects any act or condition, producing any number
of negative effects to those affected (pain, crying blood, inability to
speak…); or if cursing a primary or secondary ability, a -80 penalty every
time it is used
Arcane: 180 MR, Any level of complexity imaginable and produces major
effects, such as death, to those who fall within it’s consequences.
Maintenance: [10 / 20 / 25 / 35] Daily
Closed Paths: None
Friendship
Level: 40-50 Action: Active Type: Spiritual
Effect: Creates a bond of friendship towards another person designated by
the caster. Keep in mind the victim does not become stupid, so they do not
blindly obey their ‘friend’, but instead act according to their personality.
To overcome the effects of this spell, a successful MR or PsR check is
required. Subsequently, the resistance roll is repeated only once a day, or
when something may cast doubt upon the companionship this spell creates.

Send Dreams
Level: 40-50 Action: Active Type: Automatic
Effect: Allows the caster to send images and words to the subconscious of
a sleeper. This does not work as a communication system, since the caster
will have no record of what the dreamer says or does. The messages can be
sent to any subject, regardless of distance. The only conditions needed to
be affected is to be both asleep and chosen by the caster.
Casting Level Base Intermediate Advanced Arcane
Zeon 120 180 240 300
Int. Required 8 10 12 15
Base: The dream is vague and not very illuminating, allowing only
fragmented images, disjointed phrases and sounds.
Intermediate: The dream is value, but is allowed to show sequences and
places to the dreamer.
Advanced: The dreams are clear and convey an understandable message,
as well as images and sequences devised by the caster.
Arcane: The dreams are completely clear and the dreamer has a vague
awareness similar to that of the caster, allowing the dreamer to even ask
questions or interact with what they see in the dream.
Maintenance: None
Closed Paths: None

Acid Cloud
Level: 50-60 Action: Active Type: Automatic
Effect: Forms a corrosive cloud, which melts any material by mere
contact. All bodies in contact with the cloud must succeed at a PhR check
or lose an amount of life points equal to the level of failure. If an object
fails this check by 50 points or more, it loses 1 level of quality. Each turn
that a body is exposed to the cloud and fails the associated resistance
check, it applies a cumulative -10 penalty to subsequent checks.
The caster can move the cloud at a speed determined by the level of the
spell. All bodies inside it’s area of effect the turn after it is formed are
affected. Naturally, it is not possible to distinguish targets inside the spell’s
area, including the caster themselves.

Merge With Body
Level: 50-60 Action: Active Type: Effect
Effect: Merges one or more objects such as weapons, armor, lock-picks,
etc. with the individual designated by the caster, with a presence limit
determined by the level of the spell. The object remains hidden under any
visible form desired, such as a scar or tattoo, until such time as the person
containing it decides to remove it. While hidden, the carrier will not have
any penalty applied for the object’s weight or size, but cannot use it for it’s
benefits. It is not possible to merge something that exceeds more than ten
times the size of the person acting as the container.
Separating the object and externalizing it requires a full combat turn.

Plague
Level: 60-70 Action: Active Type: Automatic
Effect: Causes a disease to spread endemically. In Game terms, this spell
must be cast on a subject or place infected with a disease no greater than
the limit imposed by the level of the spell, and by doing so automatically
spreads it to all individuals within it’s area of effect. The people affected
are entitled to a DR against the disease to avoid it’s effects. The only
condition to be affected is to be within the area when it is cast.
Casting Level Base Intermediate Advanced Arcane
Zeon 140 200 240 280
Int. Required 8 11 13 16
Base: 20 Disease level limit, 1km Radius.
Intermediate: 40 Disease level limit, 5km Radius.
Advanced: 60 Disease level limit, 10km Radius.
Arcane: 80 Disease level limit, 25km Radius, the difficulty of the DR is
increased by 10 points.
Maintenance: None
Closed Paths: Illusion
Rejection
Level: 60-70 Action: Active Type: Effect
Effect: Enchants a physical body with magical energy, producing a strong
rejection to anyone who touches it. The spell causes an impact on anyone
coming into contact with the subject, and the spell has it’s own Strength
value for this purpose. As in similar scenarios, the GM must determine the
damage suffered by the character pushed by the impact, depending on the
difference in opposed check and the elements in the environment. The
maximum presence of the affected subject cannot exceed the limit set by
the level of the spell.

Level 70-80
Containment
Level: 70-80 Action: Active Type: Automatic
Effect: Enchants a certain place by making any creature that enters or is
already inside unable to leave it’s border unless they succeed at an MR
check. Although they can move within the interior area freely, it will be
completely impossible to cross outside the barrier. Once a subject fails to
resist, they are not entitled to any new resistance check for as long as the
spell is maintained, unless for some reason their resistance score increases.
The only condition to be affected is simply to be inside the area any time
after the turn it is cast. For this reason, the caster will be unable to choose
targets affected in the area, and will themselves be contained if they remain
in the area.

Deflect Trajectory
Level: 70-80 Action: Passive Type: Defense, Effect
Effect: Modifies the trajectory of a moving body, redirecting it in the way
the caster wishes. Affects any amount of mass, whether small like an arrow
or as large as a moving ship. Keep in mind it does not allow movement of
a static body, but only modifying the route that one already in motion
takes.
This may also be used as a defense against material attacks, such as slashes
or projectiles. In the event the caster achieves a successful defense with
magic projection, they may redirect the attack to another foe.
The effects of this spell in either case can be avoided by a successful MR
check.

Detection Mark
Level: 70-80 Action: Active Type: Effect, Spiritual
Effect: Enchants a person, object, or place, allowing the caster to attune
their senses to it. In this way, the caster may use their perception abilities
as if present with the subject, as long as their distance is no greater than the
limit set by the level of the spell. In addition, the caster is always aware of
where the mark is in relation to their own location.
Living beings and supernatural objects being targeted by this spell may
attempt an MR check to avoid it’s effects.

Perfect Target
Level: 70-80 Action: Active Type: Effect
Effect: Magically enchants a conventional projectile so that, once shot, it
will reach its target inescapably. The projectile still uses the ability of it’s
launcher, and if used to attack an individual, it is defended against using
the conventional rules for projectile defense. However, the supernatural
spell’s power gives the attacker a bonus to their roll, allowing them to
ignore any penalty of Anima: Beyond Fantasy – Table 44: Projectile
Modifiers, as well as distance to the target. In other words, it will always
reach it’s target, no matter where the launcher is. Perfect Target must be
used in the same combat turn as the projectile is launched.

Maintenance: None

Stall Spell
Level: 70-80 Action: Active Type: Effect
Effect: Allows a second spell to be ‘stored’ inside an object, so that it may
be used by other sorcerers. Stall spell must be cast at the same time as the
intended spell, so that the second spell instead of functioning normally,
will be stuck inside something. The stalled spell may be activated later, by
any sorcerer who enters the matching Zeonic cost into the container as
when it was cast. The holder of the object will control the spell as if they
were the caster, and may even pay it’s maintenance if applicable.
The stalled spell may be used more than once, provided the holder pays the
same cost each time. As an example; an archmage has placed a fireball
spell at Base grade inside of a cane. From that moment on, any character
that possesses that cane may pay the 50 Zeon to trigger a fireball.

Arcane: 240 Zeon limit
Maintenance: None

Eliminate Needs
Level: 80-90 Action: Active Type: Effect
Effect: Completely eliminates any physical needs of an individual. In
game terms, as long as the spell is maintained, the subject will not be
affected by the effects of hunger, fatigue, or sleep, completely ignoring any
penalties caused by them (or by their lack of them). The character can
continue to spend fatigue points to increase their physical actions, and are
recovered at a rate of 1 point per hour passively, and does not suffer
negative effects from very low fatigue points.

both directions, or only transports one way. The portals can only transmit a
maximum amount of presence per day; this value, along with the size of
the portal opening as well as the maximum distance that can separate the
points are determined by the level of the spell.

Prepare Spell
Level: 80-90 Action: Active Type: Effect
Effect: Allows the caster to introduce a second spell up to the Zeon limit
set by this spell inside an object or individual, to cast automatically in the
future, without accumulation or preparation. This and the second spell
must be cast at the same time, so that instead of functioning normally, the
second spell remains to be used later. The prepared spell may be activated
by the caster’s will, the will of the person it is being stored in, or the one in
possession of the enchanted object. It uses the magic projection of the
original caster, unless the second person or holder of the object intends to
use their own. Once cast, the prepared spell disappears.
If this spell’s maintenance cost ceases to be paid, the prepared spell is also
lost. It is only possible to use one prepared spell per combat turn,
regardless of the amount stored. Any object or person can hold up to four
times their presence in Zeonic value of prepared spells. That is, an
individual of 50 presence can hold up to 400 Zeon worth of prepared spells
stored inside them.

Level 90-100
Conditioning
Level: 90-100 Action: Active Type: Effect
Effect: Causes other spells used by the caster to halt until a predetermined
circumstance. This spell must be cast at the same time as the conditioned
spells, such that instead of functioning normally, the conditioned spells
will wait to be activated until the circumstance the caster has designated.
Each spell may be subject to a different condition, but once chosen, cannot
be changed later.
The spell will be activated at the same instant the condition occurs, even if
the caster themselves is not aware of it. For example, the caster may
condition a Stop Fall spell to the circumstance of ‘falling from great
height’, or a necromancer may condition the spell Surpass Death to ‘the
moment I die’. The spell itself remains either Active or Passive as the
normal rules state, but always end up being used at the end of the combat
turn in which the condition is met if the caster cannot act in the turn the
condition happens.
The conditioned spells require no maintenance as they are not active, but if
this spell ceases to be maintained, the conditioned spells are also lost. As
many spells can be conditioned as desired, so long as each spell does not
exceed the maximum Zeonic value determined by the level of this spell.
A character can only be affected by one Conditioning spell at a time.

Int. Required 11 13 15 17

Advanced: 200 Zeon limit
Arcane: 250 Zeon limit
Maintenance: [30 / 40 / 50 / 60] Daily

Link Maintenance
Level: 90-100 Action: Active Type: Spiritual
Effect: Allows the caster to link the maintenance payment for one of their
spells to another individual or object, forcing the spending of the new
target’s Zeon in an unconscious way. This link can only be used on people
who possess the Gift, or upon magical beings or objects, which can avoid
being affected with a successful MR check. If the linked individual does
not realize they are being targeted by this spell, they are not entitled to a
new resistance check. If noticed, however, a new resistance check is able
to be performed depending on the type of spell linked. In the case of daily
maintenance spells a check is allowed each day, and for all others a new
check is allowed every five rounds. The moment that a resistance is
successful or if the linked target runs out of Zeon, the effect of the link
disappears forever.

Possession
Level: 90-100 Action: Active Type: Spiritual
Effect: Allows the caster to take control of the body of another individual,
temporarily moving a portion of their soul into the host. This dominion
allows the caster to either use the abilities of the possessed individual, or
their own (this is chosen each combat turn to use one or the other, in no
case may they use both). In the case that the host body’s abilities are
chosen, the physical characteristics will be that of the host, and not the
caster. If the individual does not have a physical form similar to that of the
caster, they will apply a -20 to -40 penalty, at the GM’s discretion, due to
the difficulty in controlling the new body correctly. While this spell is
maintained, the caster’s original body remains in a coma. Attacks unable to
damage energy will only affect the host body, but if they are supernatural,
both the host and the caster receive the full damage. If the host body is a
damage resistant creature, the caster suffers only one tenth the damage that
the host body receives in this case. In the event the host body dies, but the
caster still has life points remaining, the soul immediately returns to the
caster’s body. To avoid the effects of this spell, it is necessary to succeed at
an MR check. This check may be repeated once per day that passes, or
every time the victim take an action completely against their nature.
Casting Level Base Intermediate Advanced Arcane
Zeon 300 400 500 600
Int. Required 10 12 14 16
Base: 120 MR
Intermediate: 140 MR
Advanced: 180 MR
Arcane: 220 MR
Maintenance: [30 / 40 / 50 / 60] Daily
Closed Paths: None
Predestination
Level: 90-100 Action: Active Type: Automatic
Effect: Through this spell the magician can modify the coming events
according to their desire, predestining different events in the future. The
caster may designate the complexity of these events in any way they wish.
It is possible that they preordain one event, leaving to chance the many
small events that lead to it’s realization, or on the contrary determine each
of these aspects leading to the ultimate end. Preordained events may be set
for a specific date or may be scheduled to repeat eternally throughout time
– Solstices in leap years for example.
Although it is not intended for this spell to cause a completely impossible
result, the possibilities are limited only by the imagination of the caster.
Given the enormous complexity of this spell, some examples are provided
to demonstrate it’s uses. An approximate predestination would be for the
caster to designate that misfortune befall a family and all their offspring,
condemning their line to a horrible death before reaching a certain age. On
the other hand, should the caster wish to be more explicit, they could
condemn the first born of this family to be murdered by wild wolves on the
very night of their birth. Naturally, not all predestination need be negative;
the caster may wish to predestine a child to become king.
However, the future created by this spell is not absolute and inevitable;
there is always a chance of its prevention from coming true. Anyone
outside the limits preordained by the caster, that is, anyone whom is
outside those that must attempt an MR check, has the ability to keep
destiny from being fulfilled. In order to neutralize the actions of any third
parties, the caster may plan ahead and add security measures within the
limits of the spell. A good example of this would be the inclusion of a
clause stating that any who try to thwart destiny will be murdered by
wolves. To prevent this spell from working, a single MR check is
performed by the person, creature, or object directly affected by the
predestination with the highest resistance at the time of it’s casting. Those
within it’s effects are unable to resist, and chance will play against any
decision they make to achieve the end goals. The MR check may only be
repeated when one of the predetermined events is met, so that the greater
degree of details the spell is crafted with, the greater the chances it can be
avoided. If any of the spell’s predestined events are thwarted, the entire
spell stops working. The only condition to be affected by the spell is to be
within the narrative created by the caster.

Seal
Level: 90-100 Action: Active Type: Effect
Effect: Seal an active Spiritual spell within a person, making it impossible
to be freed from the spell’s effects. This spell is used on a previously cast
Spiritual spell, successfully cast upon an individual who failed their
resistance roll. Thus, until that spell is destroyed or otherwise no longer
maintained, the affected individual loses all ability to perform a new
resistance roll, regardless of what the general rules of the associated spell
says. It has no effects on a spell without maintenance, or those that affect
an area instead of an individual.

Shield Against Powers
Level: 90-100 Action: Active Type: Automatic
Effect: The caster creates an area that prevents the use of supernatural
powers of any kind. Inside, no summon, spell, psychic power, or Ki
Technique who’s difficulty, Zeonic value, Psychic Potential, or Ki cost
exceeds what the degree of the spell indicates. This area remains stationary
to where it was cast, and affects everyone within, including the caster.

Strengthen Magic
Level: 90-100 Action: Active Type: Effect
Effect: Intensifies the power of magic used by the caster, making spells
they cast harder to destroy. While maintained, all spells of the caster are
considered to have ‘fake’ Zeon points added to the Zeonic value of their
spells.
Since these points are not real, they do not add any additional effects or
increase the casting level of the spells. They merely increase their value for
the purposes of comparing to abilities that affect and destroy spells. If, for
example, the caster uses Strengthen Magic in the base level (+50 points),
and uses the spell Create Light at base level (50 Zeonic value), the
potential of the Light spell would not be increased to any higher level, but
a second sorcerer using Destroy Magic at base grade (50 Zeon limit)
would not be able to destroy the light spell.

The Gift of Knowledge
Level: 90-100 Action: Active Type: Effect
Effect: Using magic, the caster obtains intellectual knowledge that they do
not know. This spell grants the caster or their designated target a number of
bonus points to the Intellectual field of secondary abilities, which they may
distribute as they deem appropriately. This cannot exceed 340 points
towards a single ability.
For example, if you get 100 points, you can increase the History skill by
40 points, Science by 30 points, and Animals by 30 points.
Characters can only benefit from one Gift of Knowledge spell at a time.

The Magistrate
Level: 90-100 Action: Active Type: Automatic
Effect: This spell creates an area in which the caster becomes an absolute
judge of what happens, obtaining the ability to intervene in the decisions of
all present. If desired, they can veto any active action of those inside the
area, prohibiting them from doing anything the caster doesn’t wish for
them to do. The caster is unable to subject anyone to their direct control,
but can prevent them from acting freely. This intervention is a passive
action, so it interrupts even those who have won the initiative roll. The
caster must be aware of any action for them to veto it. Every time a
character wants to perform an act that is prohibited by the caster, they must
succeed at an MR check, or will not be able to perform the action.
However, even if someone manages to resist, they are not totally free from
the spell, since in the next round they will still submit to the will of the
magistrate if they do not resist again.
This spell remains stationary to the area where cast, and the only condition
to be affected is to be within the area.
